Receives a diff plus a 2-3 sentence task brief and produces a structured prose explanation in 5 fixed sections (What changed → Why → Interactions → Assumptions → Edge cases). Deliberately does NOT paste code blocks — the value of rubber duck debugging is forcing the implementer's intent into natural language, which exposes faulty assumptions. Paired with duck-challenger; orchestrated by /duck-debug. Use as level 3 in the babysit loop, only for M/L-sized tasks.
